# Codex conversation 01a0fa4b-0ddd-7082-9d1f-c1fa373f05cb

## developer

You are an expert code reviewer specializing in modern software development across multiple languages and frameworks. Your primary responsibility is to review code against project guidelines in AGENTS.md with high precision to minimize false positives.

## Review Scope

By default, review unstaged changes from `git diff`. The user may specify different files or scope to review.

## Core Review Responsibilities

**Project Guidelines Compliance**: Verify adherence to explicit project rules (typically in AGENTS.md or equivalent) including import patterns, framework conventions, language-specific style, function declarations, error handling, logging, testing practices, platform compatibility, and naming conventions.

**Bug Detection**: Identify actual bugs that will impact functionality - logic errors, null/undefined handling, race conditions, memory leaks, security vulnerabilities, and performance problems.

**Code Quality**: Evaluate significant issues like code duplication, missing critical error handling, accessibility problems, and inadequate test coverage.

## Confidence Scoring

Rate each potential issue on a scale from 0-100:

- **0**: Not confident at all. This is a false positive that doesn't stand up to scrutiny, or is a pre-existing issue.
- **25**: Somewhat confident. This might be a real issue, but may also be a false positive. If stylistic, it wasn't explicitly called out in project guidelines.
- **50**: Moderately confident. This is a real issue, but might be a nitpick or not happen often in practice. Not very important relative to the rest of the changes.
- **75**: Highly confident. Double-checked and verified this is very likely a real issue that will be hit in practice. The existing approach is insufficient. Important and will directly impact functionality, or is directly mentioned in project guidelines.
- **100**: Absolutely certain. Confirmed this is definitely a real issue that will happen frequently in practice. The evidence directly confirms this.

**Only report issues with confidence ≥ 80.** Focus on issues that truly matter - quality over quantity.

## Output Guidance

Start by clearly stating what you're reviewing. For each high-confidence issue, provide:

- Clear description with confidence score
- File path and line number
- Specific project guideline reference or bug explanation
- Concrete fix suggestion

Group issues by severity (Critical vs Important). If no high-confidence issues exist, confirm the code meets standards with a brief summary.

Structure your response for maximum actionability - developers should know exactly what to fix and why.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies them into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

It summarizes the Claude-oriented project guidance from `CLAUDE.md` in a smaller format that is fast for Codex to scan. `CLAUDE.md` remains the fuller source of project knowledge.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Fast Locator\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vault root (the `~/ai-os` directory IS the Obsidian vault): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os`\\r\\n- Code repo root (this project): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/projects/lsatu`\\r\\n- Live vault knowledge: `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/wiki/` (hubs, voice, business), `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/students/`, `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/corpus/`\\r\\n- Pre-2026-07-16 LSATU product knowledge (archived, read-only): `/Users/joshuaf/ai-os/archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/`\\r\\n\\r\\nIf an older note says `~/Documents/Obsidian Vault/...` or `~/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/LSATU`, treat it as stale — the vault was consolidated into `~/ai-os`. Use the absolute paths above.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Project Map\\r\\n\\r\\n- `src/App.tsx`: route registration and top-level app wiring.\\r\\n- `src/pages/`: route-level screens, including the Foyer and admin/teacher surfaces.\\r\\n- `src/components/`: shared UI, including `foyer/`, `inbox/`, and shadcn/ui primitives.\\r\\n- `src/hooks/`: auth, permissions, and shared data hooks.\\r\\n- `src/lib/`: drill, WAJ, gamification, and other product logic.\\r\\n- `src/integrations/supabase/`: generated Supabase client and types. Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}]

Otherwise it falls through to the ordinary\\r\\n * ProtectedRoute, which shows the locked state.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Same shape as BootcampGate, which has done this for bootcamps all along.\\r\\n * Route-level only: RLS already scopes every assignment table by student_id,\\r\\n * so this decides what a student is shown, not what they can read.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport function AssignmentGate({\\r\\n  flag,\\r\\n  authKey,\\r\\n  authorize,\\r\\n  assignmentOnly = false,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: {\\r\\n  flag: PermissionFlag | readonly PermissionFlag[];\\r\\n  /** Stable name for the thing being authorised, for caching. null = nothing to authorise. */\\r\\n  authKey: string | null;\\r\\n  authorize: (userId: string) => Promise<boolean>;\\r\\n  assignmentOnly?: boolean;\\r\\n  children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const flags = (Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : [flag]) as PermissionFlag[];\\r\\n  const flagOpen = permissions.is_admin || (!assignmentOnly && flags.some((f) => Boolean(permissions[f])));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\\"assignment-gate\\", user?.id, authKey],\\r\\n    enabled: authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey,\\r\\n    queryFn: () => authorize(user!.id),\\r\\n    staleTime: 60_000,\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  const waiting =\\r\\n    authReady && !!user && !permissions.loading && !flagOpen && !!authKey && isLoading;\\r\\n  if (waiting) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!flagOpen && assigned) return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  return <ProtectedRoute flag={assignmentOnly ? [] : flag}>{!flagOpen && assignmentOnly ? <LockedModule /> : children}</ProtectedRoute>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ── What each runner accepts as proof ──────────────────────────────────────\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function ownsRow(table: string, id: string, userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(table as never)\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"id\\", id)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return !!data;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** A live practice-test assignment for exactly this PrepTest. */\\r\\nexport async function hasPracticeTestAssignment(userId: string, pt?: number): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  let query = supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"practice_test_assignments\\" as never)\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\");\\r\\n  if (typeof pt === \\"number\\") query = query.eq(\\"pt\\", pt);\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await query.limit(1);\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return (data ?? []).length > 0;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function hasAssignedBootcamp(userId: string): Promise<boolean> {\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\\"classroom_items\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"id\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"student_id\\", userId)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"type\\", \\"bootcamp_module\\")\\r\\n    .neq(\\"status\\", \\"archived\\")\\r\\n    .limit(1);\\r\\n  if (error) return false;\\r\\n  return (data ?? `ta:${assignmentId}` : null}\\r\\n      authorize={(uid) => ownsRow(\\"ta_assignments\\", assignmentId!, uid)}\\r\\n    >\\r\\n      {children}\\r\\n    </AssignmentGate>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Permanent activity doors require that exact library assignment. */\\r\\nexport function ActivityLibraryGate({ activityId, mode, children }: {\\r\\n  activityId?: string; mode?: string; children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}) {\\r\\n  return <AssignmentGate flag=\\"has_activity_access\\" assignmentOnly\\r\\n    authKey={`activity-library:${activityId ?? A plain `npm run dev` now exercises the real permission path, so\\r\\n * a gating regression is visible locally instead of surfacing after deploy.\\r\\n * Set VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES=1 in .env.local to walk gated routes without the\\r\\n * matching flags.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * It relaxes *flags*, never authentication — the caller applies it only after\\r\\n * the signed-in check, so no configuration can make this component render to an\\r\\n * anonymous visitor.\\r\\n */\\r\\nfunction isLocalDevAccessAllowed() {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    import.meta.env.DEV &&\\r\\n    import.meta.env.VITE_DEV_BYPASS_GATES === \\"1\\" &&\\r\\n    LOCAL_DEV_HOSTS.has(window.location.hostname)\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport default function ProtectedRoute({ flag, requireAdmin, allowSignedIn, children }: ProtectedRouteProps) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const location = useLocation();\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Auth first, and nothing before it. `useUserPermissions` reports\\r\\n  // `loading: authReady && !!user && isPending`, which is *false* while auth is\\r\\n  // still resolving — so a check on `!user` alone read \\"not yet known\\" as\\r\\n  // \\"signed out\\" and bounced every cold deep link to /auth. Holding here until\\r\\n  // auth is definitively resolved is what makes an assignment link survive.\\r\\n  if (!authReady) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Authentication is intrinsic to this component: every shape of it requires a\\r\\n  // user. The check used to run only for `requireAdmin` or flagged routes, so a\\r\\n  // wrapper with `allowSignedIn` and no flag fell through to the unconditional\\r\\n  // render at the bottom and served protected content to anonymous visitors.\\r\\n  if (!user) {\\r\\n    const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\\r\\n    return <Navigate to={authUrlWithReturnTo(returnTo)} replace />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // After the auth checks, never before: the dev bypass opens locked features\\r\\n  // on a local machine, not the front door.\\r\\n  if (isLocalDevAccessAllowed()) {\\r\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Only a true cold read blocks; cached reads and background refetches keep\\r\\n  // this false, so gated navigations do not flash.\\r\\n  if (permissions.loading) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-background flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"w-6 h-6 border-2 border-muted border-t-foreground rounded-full animate-spin\\" />\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (requireAdmin) {\\r\\n    return permissions.is_admin ? <>{children}</> : <LockedModule />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (allowSignedIn) {\\r\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const flags = Array.isArray(flag) ? flag : flag ? [flag] : [];\\r\\n  if (flags.length > 0) {\\r\\n    if (permissions.is_admin || flags.some((f) => permissions[f])) {\\r\\n      return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    return <LockedModule />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"95bc58","wall_time_seconds":2.6764872,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":482,"output":"rg: src/components/BootcampGate.tsx: The system cannot find the file specified. The frontend can ship ahead of the function (edge\\r\\n * functions deploy through Lovable, separately from a publish), and an older\\r\\n * function neither returns access_tier nor knows set_tier or the practice-test\\r\\n * actions. Admins can read and write profiles directly under RLS, so each path\\r\\n * falls back to the table when the function cannot do the job.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Without the read fallback the People page would show every student as\\r\\n * \\"No tier\\", and the signup queue would offer to put all of them on Tier 3.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Fill access_tier from profiles when the list came from an older function. */\\r\\nexport async function withAccessTiers(users: ManagedUser[]): Promise<ManagedUser[]> {\\r\\n  if (users.length === 0 || users.some((u) => \'access_tier\' in u)) return users;\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'profiles\')\\r\\n    .select(\'class_id, access_tier\' as never);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const tiers = new Map(\\r\\n    ((data ?? []) as unknown as Array<{ class_id: string; access_tier: AccessTier | null }>).map((row) => [\\r\\n      row.class_id,\\r\\n      row.access_tier,\\r\\n    ]),\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  return users.map((u) => ({ ...u, access_tier: tiers.get(u.class_id) ?? null }));\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** The whole list, tiers included, from either function version. */\\r\\nexport async function listManagedUsers(): Promise<ManagedUser[]> {\\r\\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', { method: \'GET\' });\\r\\n  if (res.error) throw res.error;\\r\\n  return withAccessTiers(((res.data as ManagedUser[]) ?? []) as ManagedUser[]);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Apply and verify every preset flag. An older function can acknowledge a tier\\r\\n * while applying obsolete defaults, so its tier label alone is not proof.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport async function setStudentTier(classId: string, tier: AccessTier): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const expected = tierFlags(tier);\\r\\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\\r\\n    method: \'POST\',\\r\\n    body: { action: \'set_tier\', class_id: classId, tier },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  const written = (res.data as { profile?: Record<string, unknown> } | null)?.profile;\\r\\n  const matches = (row: Record<string, unknown> | undefined) => row?.access_tier === tier &&\\r\\n    Object.entries(expected).every(([key, value]) => row[key] === value);\\r\\n  if (!res.error && matches(written)) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const updates: Record<string, unknown> = { access_tier: tier, ...expected };\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n    .from(\'profiles\')\\r\\n    .update(updates as never)\\r\\n    .eq(\'class_id\', classId)\\r\\n    .select(\'*\');\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const row = (data ?? [])[0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\\r\\n  if (!matches(row)) throw new Error(\'Tier defaults were not saved. The access policy migration must be applied.\');\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Save an override with its dependent permissions in one admin-controlled write. */\\r\\nexport async function setStudentAccess(person: ManagedUser, field: string, value: boolean): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  if (!(CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(field)) throw new Error(\'Unknown access control\');\\r\\n  const flags = Object.fromEntries(CONFIGURABLE_FLAG_KEYS.map((key) => [key, Boolean(person[key])]));\\r\\n  const updates = withDerivedAccess({ ...flags, [field]: value });\\r\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\'profiles\').update(updates as never)\\r\\n    .eq(\'class_id\', person.class_id).select(\'*\');\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  const row = (data ?? [])[0] as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\\r\\n  if (!row || Object.entries(updates).some(([key, expected]) => row[key] !== expected)) {\\r\\n    throw new Error(\'Access override was not saved\');\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Assign or unassign one PrepTest sitting, from either function version. */\\r\\nexport async function setPracticeTestAssignment(\\r\\n  classId: string,\\r\\n  pt: number,\\r\\n  assign: boolean,\\r\\n  adminId: string | undefined,\\r\\n): Promise<void> {\\r\\n  const res = await supabase.functions.invoke(\'admin-manage-users\', {\\r\\n    method: \'POST\',\\r\\n    body: { action: assign ? \'assign_practice_test\' : \'unassign_practice_test\', class_id: classId, pt },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  if (!res.error && (res.data as { success?: boolean } | null)?.success) return;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const sourceId = `practice_test:${pt}`;\\r\\n  if (assign) {\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase.from(\'practice_test_assignments\' as never).upsert(\\r\\n      {\\r\\n        student_id: classId,\\r\\n        pt,\\r\\n        assigned_by: adminId ?? null,\\r\\n        assigned_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\r\\n        status: \'assigned\',\\r\\n      } as never,\\r\\n      { onConflict: \'student_id,pt\' },\\r\\n    );\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    const { data: existing } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n      .select(\'id\')\\r\\n      .eq(\'student_id\', classId)\\r\\n      .eq(\'type\', \'practice_test\' as never)\\r\\n      .eq(\'source_id\', sourceId)\\r\\n      .maybeSingle();\\r\\n    if (existing) {\\r\\n      const { error: updateError } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n        .update({ status: \'assigned\', visible_to_student: true } as never)\\r\\n        .eq(\'id\', (existing as { id: string }).id);\\r\\n      if (updateError) throw updateError;\\r\\n    } else {\\r\\n      const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from(\'classroom_items\').insert({\\r\\n        student_id: classId,\\r\\n        created_by: adminId ?? null,\\r\\n        type: \'practice_test\',\\r\\n        title: `PrepTest ${pt}`,\\r\\n        description: \'A full practice test: three scored sections, in order, with a break.\',\\r\\n        source_table: \'practice_test_assignments\',\\r\\n        source_id: sourceId,\\r\\n        status: \'assigned\',\\r\\n        visible_to_student: true,\\r\\n        metadata_json: { pt, href: \'/testing\' },\\r\\n      } as never);\\r\\n      if (insertError) throw insertError;\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  } else {\\r\\n    const { error } = await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\'practice_test_assignments\' as never)\\r\\n      .update({ status: \'archived\' } as never)\\r\\n      .eq(\'student_id\', classId)\\r\\n      .eq(\'pt\', pt);\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    await supabase\\r\\n      .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n      .update({ status: \'archived\' } as never)\\r\\n      .eq(\'student_id\', classId)\\r\\n      .eq(\'type\', \'practice_test\' as never)\\r\\n      .eq(\'source_id\', sourceId);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 9.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"b5983e","wall_time_seconds":2.5738709,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1245,"output":"-- Access tiers, stage 2: the tier label.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- A tier is a template. It sets the twelve switches; the switches stay what the\\r\\n-- app checks. access_tier only names which template the switches currently\\r\\n-- match, so the People page can say \\"Tier 1\\" without re-deriving it.\\r\\n--\\r\\n--   NULL    undecided: sits in the signup queue, floor access only\\r\\n--   tier_1  all twelve switches on\\r\\n--   tier_2  hand-picked switches\\r\\n--   tier_3  the floor (Classroom, Messages, Schedule) plus assigned work\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\r\\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS access_tier text;\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\r\\n  DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_check;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.profiles\\r\\n  ADD CONSTRAINT profiles_access_tier_check\\r\\n  CHECK (access_tier IS NULL OR access_tier IN (\'tier_1\', \'tier_2\', \'tier_3\'));\\r\\n\\r\\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.profiles.access_tier IS\\r\\n  \'Access template the switches match. NULL = undecided (signup queue). Label only: the has_*_access switches are what the app enforces.\';\\r\\n\\r\\n-- The tier is an admin act, like the switches it names.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.guard_profile_admin_fields()\\r\\n RETURNS trigger\\r\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\r\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\r\\nAS $function$\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF auth.uid() IS NULL OR public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN\\r\\n    RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  IF NEW.has_bootcamp_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_bootcamp_access  OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_classroom_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_classroom_access OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_analytics_access IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_analytics_access OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_schedule_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_schedule_access  OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_practice_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_practice_access  OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_drill_access     IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_drill_access     OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_waj_access       IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_waj_access       OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_journal_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_journal_access   OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_flagged_access   IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_flagged_access   OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_chat_access      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_chat_access      OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_export_access    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_export_access    OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_ta_access        IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_ta_access        OR\\r\\n     NEW.has_activity_access  IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.has_activity_access  OR\\r\\n     NEW.access_tier          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier          OR\\r\\n     NEW.archived_at          IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at          OR\\r\\n     NEW.archived_reason      IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_reason      OR\\r\\n     NEW.proficiency_level    IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.proficiency_level    OR\\r\\n     NEW.class_id             IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.class_id             THEN\\r\\n    RAISE EXCEPTION \'profiles: access flags, access tier, archive state, proficiency_level and class_id are admin-controlled\';\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  RETURN NEW;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\n-- Drift: a Tier 1 or Tier 3 label only holds while the switches match it.\\r\\n-- Flip one switch away from the template and the label becomes Tier 2. Label\\r\\n-- only, never access: the switch that was flipped is the change, this just\\r\\n-- stops the page lying about it. Lives in the database so every write path,\\r\\n-- including a bulk update, keeps the label honest.\\r\\n--\\r\\n-- A write that sets access_tier itself (set_tier, archive, reset) is trusted\\r\\n-- and left alone, which is what lets set_tier move a row between templates.\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift()\\r\\n RETURNS trigger\\r\\n LANGUAGE plpgsql\\r\\n SET search_path TO \'public\'\\r\\nAS $function$\\r\\nDECLARE\\r\\n  all_on boolean;\\r\\n  floor_only boolean;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  IF NEW.access_tier IS NULL OR NEW.access_tier = \'tier_2\' THEN\\r\\n    RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n  IF TG_OP = \'UPDATE\' AND NEW.access_tier IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.access_tier THEN\\r\\n    RETURN NEW;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  all_on :=\\r\\n    NEW.has_drill_access AND NEW.has_practice_access AND NEW.has_activity_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_bootcamp_access AND NEW.has_journal_access AND NEW.has_flagged_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_analytics_access AND NEW.has_export_access AND NEW.has_classroom_access AND\\r\\n    NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NEW.has_ta_access;\\r\\n\\r\\n  floor_only :=\\r\\n    NEW.has_classroom_access AND NEW.has_chat_access AND NEW.has_schedule_access AND NOT (\\r\\n      NEW.has_drill_access OR NEW.has_practice_access OR NEW.has_activity_access OR\\r\\n      NEW.has_bootcamp_access OR NEW.has_journal_access OR NEW.has_flagged_access OR\\r\\n      NEW.has_analytics_access OR NEW.has_export_access OR NEW.has_ta_access\\r\\n    );\\r\\n\\r\\n  IF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_1\' AND NOT all_on THEN\\r\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\r\\n  ELSIF NEW.access_tier = \'tier_3\' AND NOT floor_only THEN\\r\\n    NEW.access_tier := \'tier_2\';\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  RETURN NEW;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$function$;\\r\\n\\r\\nDROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS profiles_access_tier_drift_trg ON public.profiles;\\r\\nCREATE TRIGGER profiles_access_tier_drift_trg\\r\\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.profiles\\r\\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.profiles_access_tier_drift();\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': \'react-router-dom\';\\r\\nimport { useQuery } from \'@tanstack/react-query\';\\r\\nimport { Lock } from \'lucide-react\';\\r\\nimport { Button } from \'@/components/ui/button\';\\r\\nimport { useAuth } from \'@/contexts/AuthContext\';\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \'@/hooks/useUserPermissions\';\\r\\nimport { supabase } from \'@/integrations/supabase/client\';\\r\\nimport { bootcampIdFromRoute, classroomItemMatchesBootcamp } from \'@/lib/bootcamps\';\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface Props {\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The route prefix for this bootcamp, e.g. \\"/bootcamp/principle-flaw\\" or\\r\\n   * \\"/topics/abstract\\". Matched against either `metadata_json.bootcamp_id`\\r\\n   * (slug, see `bootcampIdOverride`) or `source_id` on a classroom_items\\r\\n   * row of type bootcamp_module.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  routePath: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Override the slug extracted from `routePath`. Default behavior strips a\\r\\n   * leading \\"/bootcamp/\\" and trailing path segments, which works for the\\r\\n   * /bootcamp/* family. New surfaces under /topics/* should pass this\\r\\n   * explicitly (e.g. routePath \\"/topics/abstract\\", bootcampIdOverride\\r\\n   * \\"abstraction\\") so classroom_items rows assigned with the legacy slug\\r\\n   * still resolve to the same gate.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  bootcampIdOverride?: string;\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Additional route paths to accept as `source_id` matches. Used when a\\r\\n   * topic has moved (legacy /bootcamp/abstraction → /topics/abstract). The\\r\\n   * current `routePath` is always matched.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths?: string[];\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The student-facing display name, used only by the locked state.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  title?: string;\\r\\n  children: ReactNode;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * Per-student bootcamp gate. Admins always pass. Students need a\\r\\n * matching `classroom_items` row of type=\'bootcamp_module\' assigned to\\r\\n * them. If no row exists, render a calm \\"Not assigned yet\\" surface.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Direct URL hits go through this gate, so a student typing\\r\\n * /bootcamp/principle-flaw won\'t see anything they weren\'t assigned.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function BootcampGate({\\r\\n  routePath,\\r\\n  bootcampIdOverride,\\r\\n  legacyRoutePaths,\\r\\n  title,\\r\\n  children,\\r\\n}: Props) {\\r\\n  const { user, authReady } = useAuth();\\r\\n  const { is_admin, loading: permsLoading } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const bootcampId =\\r\\n    bootcampIdOverride ??\\r\\n    bootcampIdFromRoute(routePath);\\r\\n  const explicitRoutePaths = [routePath, ...(legacyRoutePaths ?? [])];\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data: assigned, isLoading } = useQuery<boolean>({\\r\\n    queryKey: [\'bootcamp-gate\', user?.id, routePath, bootcampId],\\r\\n    enabled: !!user && !is_admin && !permsLoading,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\'classroom_items\')\\r\\n        .select(\'id, status, source_id, metadata_json\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'type\', \'bootcamp_module\')\\r\\n        .eq(\'student_id\', user!.id)\\r\\n        .neq(\'status\', \'archived\');\\r\\n      if (error) throw error;\\r\\n      const rows = (data ?? []) as Array<{\\r\\n        source_id: string | null;\\r\\n        metadata_json: Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n      }>;\\r\\n      return rows.some(\\r\\n        (r) =>\\r\\n          classroomItemMatchesBootcamp(r, bootcampId) ||\\r\\n          (r.source_id\\r\\n            ? explicitRoutePaths.some((path) => r.source_id === path || r.source_id?.startsWith(`${path}/`))\\r\\n            : false),\\r\\n      );\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!authReady || permsLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (is_admin || assigned) {\\r\\n    return <>{children}</>;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (isLoading) {\\r\\n    return <div className=\\"min-h-screen bg-zinc-950\\" />;\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center px-6 py-12 bg-background\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"max-w-md text-center space-y-4\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"mx-auto w-12 h-12 rounded-full bg-muted flex items-center justify-center\\">\\r\\n          <Lock className=\\"w-5 h-5 text-muted-foreground\\" strokeWidth={1.75} />\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"space-y-1.5\\">\\r\\n          <h2 className=\\"text-lg font-semibold\\">Not assigned yet</h2>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-sm text-muted-foreground leading-relaxed\\">\\r\\n            {title ? `${title} ` : \'This bootcamp \'}\\r\\n            isn\'t part of your current path. Joshua can add it from the Teacher Workspace.\\r\\n          </p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <Button variant=\\"outline\\" size=\\"sm\\" onClick={() => navigate(\'/classroom\')}>\\r\\n          Back to Classroom\\r\\n        </Button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a13165","wall_time_seconds":2.9477515,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":26,"output":"src/components/classroom\\\\home\\\\useClassroomHome.homeMessage.test.tsx:33:    has_activity_access: false,\\n"}'}]

label: string; value: number }) {\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"border border-black/10 bg-white/20 p-4\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-black/45\\">\\r\\n        {icon}\\r\\n        <span className=\\"text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em]\\">{label}</span>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"font-mono text-2xl font-semibold text-black\\">{value}</div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7e576e","wall_time_seconds":2.8716061,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1071,"output":"import { useMemo, useState } from \\"react\\";\\r\\nimport { useNavigate } from \\"react-router-dom\\";\\r\\nimport { ArrowRight, GitBranch, Send } from \\"lucide-react\\";\\r\\nimport { useUserPermissions } from \\"@/hooks/useUserPermissions\\";\\r\\nimport { AssignWorkSheet, type AssignWorkSheetItem } from \\"@/components/teacher/library/AssignWorkSheet\\";\\r\\nimport { useAdminActivities, useStudentActivities } from \\"@/features/activity-centre\\";\\r\\nimport type { Activity, ActivityAssignmentSummary } from \\"@/features/activity-centre\\";\\r\\nimport { ActivityCard } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityCard\\";\\r\\nimport { librarySortIndex } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/content\\";\\r\\nimport { slugFromTitle } from \\"@/features/activity-centre/worlds/registry\\";\\r\\nimport \\"@/features/activity-centre/activity-centre.css\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nconst GENERAL_CONDITIONALS_ID = \\"7946f46b-e7f8-4c60-b405-4ece9c409deb\\";\\r\\n\\r\\n/**\\r\\n * The Activity Centre.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * For a student this is the list of Activities assigned to them, ordered by the\\r\\n * teaching progression: the scope of text each skill works on, a word up to a\\r\\n * passage. It used to sort by status and then by seed date, which put the\\r\\n * passage-level Activity first and the word-level one near the bottom, the\\r\\n * curriculum\'s own argument backwards.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * For an admin it is also the library. Assignment-gating is right for students\\r\\n * and was wrong for the person doing the assigning: the Centre showed him \\"No\\r\\n * activities assigned yet\\" while eight authored Activities sat in the table,\\r\\n * with the only way to open or assign one being the Teacher Desk. The library\\r\\n * below is admin-only, opens each Activity through the row-free preview, and\\r\\n * assigns through the same sheet every other assign surface uses.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Each card carries the world it opens into. Eight Activities drawn as eight\\r\\n * identical rows read as one undifferentiated list, which is the wrong\\r\\n * impression when every one of them is a different place.\\r\\n */\\r\\nexport default function ActivityCentre() {\\r\\n  const navigate = useNavigate();\\r\\n  const { assignments, loading, error } = useStudentActivities();\\r\\n  const { is_admin } = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * Her activities, which is not the same list as her assignment rows.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Starting a section builds an assignment of its own -- it has to, because\\r\\n   * the row carries the Details her Coverage is scored against and cannot be\\r\\n   * written by the client (`start_section_attempt`). Listing those rows here\\r\\n   * turned one drill into a card per run: three sections done and Read, Recall,\\r\\n   * Colour appeared four times, as though Joshua had assigned it four times.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * An Attempt is not something she was assigned. It is a run of something she\\r\\n   * was assigned, and it already has a home on the door\'s section list, where\\r\\n   * it opens its own Report Card. The door is the card; the runs are inside it.\\r\\n   *\\r\\n   * Filtered here rather than in `listStudentAssignments`, because the door\\r\\n   * reads the same hook and needs exactly the rows this page is dropping.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const ordered = useMemo(\\r\\n    () =>\\r\\n      assignments\\r\\n        .filter((a) => a.mode !== \\"section\\")\\r\\n        .sort(\\r\\n          (a, b) => librarySortIndex(slugFromTitle(a.title)) - librarySortIndex(slugFromTitle(b.title)),\\r\\n        ),\\r\\n    [assignments],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  /**\\r\\n   * The door is already on her list when the library was assigned to her, and\\r\\n   * that card leads to the same place. Only the unassigned case needs a way in.\\r\\n   */\\r\\n  const hasDoorAssignment = useMemo(\\r\\n    () => ordered.some((a) => a.mode === \\"section-library\\"),\\r\\n    [ordered],\\r\\n  );\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    // No back link of its own: the classroom rail is the way back, and it was\\r\\n    // pointing at /foyer, which only redirects to /classroom anyway. One name\\r\\n    // for this surface too, not \\"Activity Centre\\" over \\"Your activities\\".\\r\\n    <div className=\\"activity-centre\\">\\r\\n      <div className=\\"mx-auto max-w-5xl\\">\\r\\n        <header className=\\"max-w-2xl\\">\\r\\n          <h1 className=\\"ac-display text-4xl text-foreground\\">Activities</h1>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-2 text-base leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground\\">\\r\\n            Coach-led skill exercises, ordered by how much text each one works on: a single word\\r\\n            first, a whole passage last.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f2ef34","wall_time_seconds":2.9056466,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":689,"output":"                        reaching any of them from the rail swapped the whole\\r\\n                        frame: a different sidebar, a different item set, and\\r\\n                        each page\'s own header on top. Cached data keeps `loading` false, so neither flashes.\\r\\n    staleTime: 5 * 60_000,\\r\\n    gcTime: 30 * 60_000,\\r\\n    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,\\r\\n    // Retry transient failures a couple times, but never retry a dead session\\r\\n    // (that path signs the user out).\\r\\n    retry: (count, err) =>\\r\\n      !(err instanceof SessionExpiredError) && !(err instanceof AccountArchivedError) && count < 2,\\r\\n    queryFn: async () => {\\r\\n      const userId = user!.id;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Pre-check: is the session still alive on the client?\\r\\n      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession();\\r\\n      if (!session) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Fetch access flags from profile\\r\\n      const { data: profile, error: profileErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"profiles\\")\\r\\n        .select([...ACCESS_FLAGS, \\"archived_at\\"].join(\\", \\"))\\r\\n        .eq(\\"class_id\\", userId)\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(profileErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n      // A non-session read failure (transient network / 5xx) must NOT be\\r\\n      // silently converted to all-false permissions; throw so TanStack Query\\r\\n      // records the error and retries instead of caching bogus denied flags.\\r\\n      if (profileErr) throw profileErr;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Fetch admin role\\r\\n      const { data: roleData, error: roleErr } = await supabase\\r\\n        .from(\\"user_roles\\")\\r\\n        .select(\\"role\\")\\r\\n        .eq(\\"user_id\\", userId)\\r\\n        .eq(\\"role\\", \\"admin\\")\\r\\n        .maybeSingle();\\r\\n      if (isSessionDeadError(roleErr)) throw new SessionExpiredError();\\r\\n      if (roleErr) throw roleErr;\\r\\n\\r\\n      const profileRow = (profile ?? null) as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | null;\\r\\n      // Archived: no flags, no app. Sign them out on the spot.\\r\\n      if (profileRow?.archived_at) throw new AccountArchivedError();\\r\\n\\r\\n      const flagValues = { ...DEFAULT_PERMISSIONS };\\r\\n      for (const flag of ACCESS_FLAGS) {\\r\\n        flagValues[flag] = Boolean(profileRow?.[flag]);\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      Object.assign(flagValues, withDerivedAccess(flagValues));\\r\\n      flagValues.is_admin = !!roleData;\\r\\n\\r\\n      // Presence is owned by usePresenceHeartbeat; this read stays read-only.\\r\\n      // Writing last_seen_at here produced a write per permissions fetch and\\r\\n      // (with profiles in the realtime publication) a self-sustaining\\r\\n      // fetch -> write -> event -> fetch loop.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n      return flagValues;\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n\\r\\n  // A dead session bounces to /auth once. Preserve lsatu_saved_email so\\r\\n  // re-login is smooth after an expiry bounce, and carry where they were: a\\r\\n  // bare \\"/auth\\" discarded the pathname, query, and hash, so a student whose\\r\\n  // token expired on an assignment link lost the link as well as the session.\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (query.error instanceof AccountArchivedError) {\\r\\n      (async () => {\\r\\n        await signOut({ keepSavedEmail: true });\\r\\n        navigate(\\"/auth\\", { replace: true, state: { reason: \\"account_archived\\" } });\\r\\n      })();\\r\\n    } else if (query.error instanceof SessionExpiredError) {\\r\\n      const returnTo = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${location.hash}`;\\r\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Reviewed the latest tier implementation against AGENTS.md and the confirmed access rules.

No remaining high-confidence findings. Earlier issues are fixed:

- Assigned bootcamp and practice-test review pages open correctly.
- Concurrent toggles no longer overwrite unrelated access.

## assistant

Home itself is guarded: Tier 3 sees a locked page, and My Work routes require Classroom.

Two relevant findings:

- Assigned Activities disappear from Tier 1/2 Home because its filter still checks the retired activity flag. Use Classroom access instead.
- The existing mobile section overlay can expose assignment metadata from a locked Classroom detail URL. Guard the work/notes overlay with Classroom access.

The teaching text is drawn from Intro to LR Lesson 2. */\\r\\nexport default function StudentLibraryLessons() {\\r\\n  const permissions = useUserPermissions();\\r\\n  const canDrill = !permissions.loading && (permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_drill_access);\\r\\n  const canSeeProgress = !permissions.loading && (permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_analytics_access);\\r\\n  const canSeeBootcamp = !permissions.loading && (permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_bootcamp_access);\\r\\n  const canSeeActivities = !permissions.loading && (permissions.is_admin || permissions.has_activity_access);\\r\\n\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <div className=\\"min-w-0 space-y-6\\">\\r\\n      <section className=\\"overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card\\" aria-labelledby=\\"library-topic-title\\">\\r\\n        <div className=\\"border-b border-border bg-[var(--classroom-drill-page)] px-5 py-6 sm:px-8 sm:py-8\\">\\r\\n          <p className=\\"text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-muted-foreground\\">Logical Reasoning · Written lesson</p>\\r\\n          <h2 id=\\"library-topic-title\\" className=\\"mt-2 font-serif text-3xl text-foreground sm:text-4xl\\">Main Conclusion</h2>\\r\\n          <p className=\\"mt-2 max-w-2xl text-sm leading-6 text-foreground\\">Find the author’s point by separating the claim from the reasons offered for it.</p>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"grid gap-6 px-5 py-6 sm:px-8 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1.5fr)_minmax(230px,1fr)]\\">\\r\\n          <div className=\\"min-w-0 space-y-5\\">\\r\\n            <div>\\r\\n              <h3 className=\\"text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground\\">Joshua’s rule</h3>\\r\\n              <p className=\\"mt-2 font-serif text-xl leading-relaxed text-foreground\\">First decide whether the stimulus is an argument at all. If it is, separate the supporting reasons from the claim they are trying to prove.</p>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <div>\\r\\n              <h3 className=\\"text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground\\">Worked example</h3>\\r\\n              <blockquote className=\\"mt-2 rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-4 font-serif text-base leading-relaxed text-foreground\\">“The city should extend library hours, because evening attendance has increased steadily for three years and nearby branches with longer hours serve more students after school.”</blockquote>\\r\\n              <dl className=\\"mt-4 grid gap-3 text-sm sm:grid-cols-2\\">\\r\\n                <div className=\\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3\\"><dt className=\\"font-semibold\\">Conclusion</dt><dd className=\\"mt-1\\">The city should extend library hours.</dd></div>\\r\\n                <div className=\\"rounded-lg bg-muted/50 p-3\\"><dt className=\\"font-semibold\\">Supporting reasons</dt><dd className=\\"mt-1\\">Evening attendance has increased; nearby branches with longer hours serve more students after school.</dd></div>\\r\\n              </dl>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <p className=\\"text-xs text-muted-foreground\\">From Intro to LR, Lesson 2: Argument Anatomy.</p>\\r\\n          </div>\\r\\n          <aside className=\\"min-w-0 space-y-4 border-t border-border pt-5 lg:border-l lg:border-t-0 lg:pl-6 lg:pt-0\\" aria-label=\\"Main Conclusion next steps\\">\\r\\n            <div>\\r\\n              <h3 className=\\"font-semibold text-foreground\\">Try the idea</h3>\\r\\n              <p className=\\"mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground\\">Practice Main Conclusion questions, then review your first answers here.</p>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            {canDrill ?
